20: class ClipboardTextMixin:
21:     """Mixin: copy/paste text client <-> host."""
22: 
23:     def clear_local_and_notify_peers(self):
24:         """Xóa clipboard máy này và báo client/agent xóa theo."""
25:         self.pending_remote_files = []
26:         self.dummy_h_active = False
27:         hwnd = getattr(self, "cached_app_hwnd", None)
28:         if not hwnd and getattr(self, "listener", None):
29:             hwnd = getattr(self.listener, "hwnd", None)
30:         try:
31:             clear_local_clipboard(hwnd)
32:         except Exception as e:
33:             log_debug(f"[clear_local_and_notify_peers] local: {e}")
34:         if self.app and getattr(self.app, "is_headless", False):
35:             try:
36:                 threading.Thread(target=self._send_to_pipe, args=("CLEAR", ""), daemon=True).start()
37:             except Exception:
38:                 pass
39:         try:
40:             pkt = json.dumps({"type": "clear_clipboard"}).encode("utf-8")
41:             socks = set()
42:             try:
43:                 socks.update(self.active_sockets)
44:             except Exception:
45:                 pass
46:             try:
47:                 socks.update(socket_passwords.keys())
48:             except Exception:
49:                 pass
50:             for conn in list(socks):
51:                 try:
52:                     pw = socket_passwords.get(conn)
53:                     if pw is not None:
54:                         send_msg(conn, pkt, pw)
55:                 except Exception:
56:                     pass
57:         except Exception as e:
58:             log_debug(f"[clear_local_and_notify_peers] notify: {e}")
59: 
60:     def _host_text_poll_loop(self):
61:         """Backup: worker poll copy trên host khi agent/WM_CLIPBOARDUPDATE miss."""
62:         last_seq = 0
63:         while True:
64:             time.sleep(0.35)
65:             try:
66:                 if not self.active_sockets:
67:                     last_seq = 0
68:                     continue
69:                 if getattr(self, "dummy_h_active", False):
70:                     continue
71:                 if getattr(self, "is_rendering", False) or self.transfer_in_progress:
72:                     continue
73:                 if is_own_clipboard_write():
74:                     continue
75:                 seq = get_clipboard_sequence_number()
76:                 if not seq or seq == last_seq:
77:                     continue
78:                 if seq == getattr(self, "_last_sent_clip_seq", 0):
79:                     continue
80:                 from utils.clipboard_api import clipboard_has_file_formats, get_clipboard_files
81:                 if clipboard_has_file_formats():
82:                     files = get_clipboard_files(retries=1)
83:                     if files:
84:                         last_seq = seq
85:                         self._process_clipboard_change(provided_files=files, force=True)
86:                         continue
87:                 text = get_clipboard_text()
88:                 if not text:
89:                     last_seq = seq
90:                     continue
91:                 if self._forward_clipboard_text(text):
92:                     last_seq = seq
93:                     print("[Clipboard] Host poll: phát hiện text mới, gửi sang client.")
94:             except Exception:
95:                 pass
96: 
97:     def try_forward_local_clipboard_text(self, owner_hwnd=None):
98:         """Local copy text (không có file trên clipboard) → gửi sang peer."""
99:         current_text = get_clipboard_text(owner_hwnd)
100:         if not current_text:
101:             return False
102:         self.pending_remote_files = []
103:         self._reoffer_files = None
104:         self._allow_delayed = False
105:         if self._forward_clipboard_text(current_text):
106:             log_debug(f"[Clipboard] Phát hiện text clipboard mới locally: {current_text[:50]}...")
107:             print("[Clipboard] Đang gửi text clipboard sang đối tác...")
108:             return True
109:         return False
110: 
111:     def _forward_clipboard_text(self, text):
112:         """Gửi text clipboard sang viewer/host. Cùng nội dung copy lần 2 vẫn gửi (theo sequence)."""
113:         if not text or not self.active_sockets:
114:             return False
115:         if is_own_clipboard_write():
116:             return False
117:         seq = get_clipboard_sequence_number()
118:         if seq and seq == getattr(self, "_last_sent_clip_seq", 0):
119:             return False
120:         pkt = json.dumps({"type": "clipboard_text", "text": text}).encode("utf-8")
121:         sent = False
122:         with self.lock:
123:             sockets_to_remove = []
124:             for s in list(self.active_sockets):
125:                 try:
126:                     send_msg(s, pkt)
127:                     sent = True
128:                 except Exception:
129:                     sockets_to_remove.append(s)
130:             for s in sockets_to_remove:
131:                 if s in self.active_sockets:
132:                     self.active_sockets.remove(s)
133:         if sent:
134:             self.last_sent_text = text
135:             if seq:
136:                 self._last_sent_clip_seq = seq
137:         return sent
138: 
139:     def _apply_remote_clipboard_text(self, text):
140:         """Ghi CF_UNICODETEXT trên thread của clipboard listener.
141: 
142:         ClipPkt gọi OpenClipboard(Tk hwnd) từ thread khác → fail, clipboard trống,
143:         Ctrl+V trên Host chỉ ding một tiếng.
144:         """
145:         self.pending_remote_files = []
146:         self._reoffer_files = None
147:         self.dummy_h_active = False
148:         hwnd = getattr(self.listener, "hwnd", None) if self.listener else None
149:         if hwnd:
150:             self._pending_set_text = text
151:             self.ignore_destroy_clipboard = True
152:             try:
153:                 ctypes.windll.user32.SendMessageW(
154:                     ctypes.c_void_p(hwnd), 0x0400 + 102, 0, 0
155:                 )
156:                 log_debug("[_apply_remote_clipboard_text] SendMessage WM_SET_CLIP_TEXT xong")
157:                 self._allow_delayed = False
158:                 return
159:             except Exception as e:
160:                 log_debug(f"[_apply_remote_clipboard_text] SendMessage: {e}")
161:             self.ignore_destroy_clipboard = False
162:         self.ignore_destroy_clipboard = True
163:         try:
164:             ok = set_clipboard_text(text, None)
165:             log_debug(f"[_apply_remote_clipboard_text] fallback set_clipboard_text={ok}")
166:             if ok:
167:                 self._allow_delayed = False
168:         finally:
169:             self.ignore_destroy_clipboard = False
170: 
171:     def handle_clear_clipboard_packet(self, packet=None):
172:         """Peer yêu cầu xóa clipboard (type=clear_clipboard)."""
173:         del packet  # reserved
174:         self.pending_remote_files = []
175:         self.dummy_h_active = False
176:         hwnd = getattr(self, "cached_app_hwnd", None)
177:         if not hwnd and getattr(self, "listener", None):
178:             hwnd = getattr(self.listener, "hwnd", None)
179:         try:
180:             clear_local_clipboard(hwnd)
181:         except Exception as e:
182:             log_debug(f"[handle_clear_clipboard_packet] {e}")
183:         if self.app and getattr(self.app, "is_headless", False):
184:             try:
185:                 threading.Thread(target=self._send_to_pipe, args=("CLEAR", ""), daemon=True).start()
186:             except Exception:
187:                 pass
188:         print("[Clipboard] Đã xóa clipboard theo yêu cầu đối tác.")
189: 
190:     def handle_clipboard_text_packet(self, packet):
191:         """Nhận clipboard_text từ peer → ghi local (hoặc pipe agent khi headless)."""
192:         text = packet.get("text", "") if isinstance(packet, dict) else ""
193:         log_debug(f"[handle_clipboard_text_packet] Nhận clipboard_text: {str(text)[:50]}...")
194:         print("[Clipboard] Đã nhận được text clipboard từ remote. Đang cập nhật...")
195:         self.last_received_text = text
196:         if self.app and getattr(self.app, "is_headless", False):
197:             threading.Thread(target=self._send_to_pipe, args=("TEXT", text), daemon=True).start()
198:             log_debug("[handle_clipboard_text_packet] HEADLESS: gửi text qua Named Pipe cho Clipboard Agent.")
199:         else:
200:             self._apply_remote_clipboard_text(text)

107: def set_clipboard_dword_format(cf_format, value):
108:     try:
109:         kernel32 = ctypes.windll.kernel32
110:         hMem = fn_GlobalAlloc(0x0002, 4) # GMEM_MOVEABLE = 0x0002
111:         if hMem:
112:             ptr = fn_GlobalLock(hMem)
113:             if ptr:
114:                 ctypes.memmove(ptr, ctypes.byref(wintypes.DWORD(value)), 4)
115:                 fn_GlobalUnlock(hMem)
116:                 if not fn_SetClipboardData(cf_format, hMem):
117:                     fn_GlobalFree(hMem)
118:                     msg = f"[set_dword_data] Thất bại SetClipboardData cho format {cf_format}"
119:                     if is_agent_process: print(f"[ClipboardAgent] {msg}", flush=True)
120:                     log_debug(msg)
121:                     return False
122:                 else:
123:                     msg = f"[set_dword_data] Đã thiết lập format {cf_format} = {value}"
124:                     if is_agent_process: print(f"[ClipboardAgent] {msg}", flush=True)
125:                     log_debug(msg)
126:                     return True
127:             else:
128:                 fn_GlobalFree(hMem)
129:                 msg = f"[set_dword_data] GlobalLock thất bại cho format {cf_format}"

100: def _parse_files_pipe_payload(paths_str):
101:     """FILES: [paste_id|]path|path — paste_id để bỏ gói trễ sau Hủy."""
102:     parts = [p for p in (paths_str or "").split("|") if p]
103:     paste_id = None
104:     if parts and parts[0].isdigit():
105:         paste_id = int(parts[0])
106:         parts = parts[1:]
107:     return paste_id, parts

111: def create_named_pipe_with_everyone_dacl():
112:     """
113:     Tạo Named Pipe Server với Security Descriptor cho phép nhóm Everyone 
114:     có quyền Read/Write. TUYỆT ĐỐI KHÔNG truyền None vào Security Attributes.
115:     """
116:     import win32pipe
117:     import win32file
118:     import win32security
119:     import ntsecuritycon as con
120:     
121:     # Tạo Security Descriptor với DACL cho Everyone
122:     sd = win32security.SECURITY_DESCRIPTOR()
123:     sd.Initialize()
124:     
125:     # Tạo DACL
126:     dacl = win32security.ACL()
127:     dacl.Initialize()
128:     
129:     # Lấy SID của nhóm "Everyone"
130:     everyone_sid = win32security.CreateWellKnownSid(win32security.WinWorldSid)
131:     
132:     # Thêm quyền Read/Write cho Everyone
133:     dacl.AddAccessAllowedAce(
134:         win32security.ACL_REVISION,
135:         con.FILE_GENERIC_READ | con.FILE_GENERIC_WRITE,
136:         everyone_sid
137:     )
138:     
139:     sd.SetSecurityDescriptorDacl(True, dacl, False)
140:     
141:     # Tạo Security Attributes
142:     sa = win32security.SECURITY_ATTRIBUTES()
143:     sa.bInheritHandle = False
144:     sa.SECURITY_DESCRIPTOR = sd
145:     
146:     # Tạo Named Pipe
147:     pipe_handle = win32pipe.CreateNamedPipe(
148:         CLIPBOARD_PIPE_NAME,
149:         win32pipe.PIPE_ACCESS_OUTBOUND,                    # Server chỉ ghi (outbound)
150:         win32pipe.PIPE_TYPE_MESSAGE | win32pipe.PIPE_WAIT,  # Message mode, blocking
151:         4,       # Số instance tối đa
152:         10 * 1024 * 1024,    # Output buffer size (10MB)
153:         10 * 1024 * 1024,    # Input buffer size (10MB)
154:         0,       # Default timeout
155:         sa       # Security Attributes với DACL cho Everyone
156:     )
157:     
158:     return pipe_handle
159:

161: def _low_il_everyone_sa():
162:     """SECURITY_ATTRIBUTES: Everyone + Mandatory Label Low — user Medium IL ghi được."""
163:     import win32security
164:     sa = win32security.SECURITY_ATTRIBUTES()
165:     sa.bInheritHandle = 0
166:     try:
167:         sd = win32security.ConvertStringSecurityDescriptorToSecurityDescriptor(
168:             "D:(A;;GA;;;WD)(A;;GA;;;AN)S:(ML;;NW;;;LW)",
169:             win32security.SDDL_REVISION_1,
170:         )
171:         sa.SECURITY_DESCRIPTOR = sd
172:     except Exception:
173:         sd = win32security.SECURITY_DESCRIPTOR()
174:         sd.Initialize()
175:         sd.SetSecurityDescriptorDacl(True, None, False)
176:         sa.SECURITY_DESCRIPTOR = sd
177:     return sa

187: class _KBDLLHOOKSTRUCT(ctypes.Structure):
188:     _fields_ = [
189:         ("vkCode", wintypes.DWORD),
190:         ("scanCode", wintypes.DWORD),
191:         ("flags", wintypes.DWORD),
192:         ("time", wintypes.DWORD),
193:         ("dwExtraInfo", ctypes.c_size_t),
194:     ]
195: 
196: 
197: class _MSLLHOOKSTRUCT(ctypes.Structure):
198:     _fields_ = [
199:         ("pt_x", ctypes.c_long),
200:         ("pt_y", ctypes.c_long),
201:         ("mouseData", wintypes.DWORD),
202:         ("flags", wintypes.DWORD),
203:         ("time", wintypes.DWORD),
204:         ("dwExtraInfo", ctypes.c_size_t),
205:     ]
206: 
207: 
208: _HOOKPROC = ctypes.WINFUNCTYPE(LRESULT_64, ctypes.c_int, WPARAM_64, LPARAM_64)
209: _CallNextHookEx = ctypes.WINFUNCTYPE(
210:     LRESULT_64, ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.c_int, WPARAM_64, LPARAM_64
211: )(("CallNextHookEx", ctypes.windll.user32))
212: 
213: 
214: def _install_paste_ll_hooks(on_ctrl_v, on_lbutton, on_rbutton):
215:     """Hook LL chỉ cho clipboard-agent (host). Callback phải PostMessage, không OpenClipboard."""
216:     user32 = ctypes.windll.user32
217:     kernel32 = ctypes.windll.kernel32
218:     kernel32.GetModuleHandleW.restype = ctypes.c_void_p
219:     user32.SetWindowsHookExW.restype = ctypes.c_void_p
220: 
221:     def _kb(nCode, wParam, lParam):
222:         try:
223:             if nCode >= 0 and int(wParam) in (0x0100, 0x0104):
224:                 kb = ctypes.cast(int(lParam), ctypes.POINTER(_KBDLLHOOKSTRUCT)).contents
225:                 vk = int(kb.vkCode)
226:                 ctrl = bool(user32.GetAsyncKeyState(0x11) & 0x8000)
227:                 shift = bool(user32.GetAsyncKeyState(0x10) & 0x8000)
228:                 if (vk in (0x56, 0x76) and ctrl) or (vk == 0x2D and shift):
229:                     on_ctrl_v()
230:         except Exception:
231:             pass
232:         return _CallNextHookEx(None, nCode, wParam, lParam)
233: 
234:     def _mouse(nCode, wParam, lParam):
235:         try:
236:             if nCode >= 0:
237:                 msg = int(wParam)
238:                 if msg in (0x0201, 0x0203):
239:                     on_lbutton()
240:                 elif msg in (0x0204, 0x0206):
241:                     on_rbutton()
242:         except Exception:
243:             pass
244:         return _CallNextHookEx(None, nCode, wParam, lParam)
245: 
246:     kb_proc = _HOOKPROC(_kb)
247:     mouse_proc = _HOOKPROC(_mouse)
248:     hmod = kernel32.GetModuleHandleW(None)
249:     hk_kb = user32.SetWindowsHookExW(13, kb_proc, hmod, 0)
250:     hk_mouse = user32.SetWindowsHookExW(14, mouse_proc, hmod, 0)
251:     _LL_HOOK_KEEPALIVE.extend((kb_proc, mouse_proc, hk_kb, hk_mouse))
252:     return hk_kb, hk_mouse

255: class FileCopyHostMixin:
256:     """Mixin: gửi file / agent paste trên host."""

258:     def _start_uppipe_server(self):
259:         import win32pipe, win32file
260:         import time
261:         print(r"[Clipboard] UpPipe server bat dau (\\.\pipe\RemoteDesktopClipboardUpPipe).")
262:         while True:
263:             try:
264:                 sa = _low_il_everyone_sa()
265:                 pipe_handle = win32pipe.CreateNamedPipe(
266:                     r"\\.\pipe\RemoteDesktopClipboardUpPipe",
267:                     win32pipe.PIPE_ACCESS_INBOUND,
268:                     win32pipe.PIPE_TYPE_MESSAGE | win32pipe.PIPE_WAIT,
269:                     win32pipe.PIPE_UNLIMITED_INSTANCES,
270:                     65536, 65536, 0, sa
271:                 )
272:                 if pipe_handle != -1:
273:                     win32pipe.ConnectNamedPipe(pipe_handle, None)
274:                     threading.Thread(target=self._handle_uppipe_client, args=(pipe_handle,), daemon=True).start()
275:             except Exception as e:
276:                 time.sleep(1)
277: 
278:     def _handle_uppipe_client(self, pipe_handle):
279:         import win32file, json
280:         try:
281:             hr, data = win32file.ReadFile(pipe_handle, 1024 * 1024)
282:             if hr == 0 and data:
283:                 raw = data.decode('utf-8').strip()
284:                 if raw.startswith("CANCEL_TRANSFER") or raw == "CANCEL:" or raw.startswith("CANCEL:"):
285:                     paste_id = None
286:                     all_jobs = True
287:                     try:
288:                         if "|" in raw:
289:                             paste_id = int(raw.split("|", 1)[1].strip())
290:                             all_jobs = False
291:                     except Exception:
292:                         paste_id = None
293:                         all_jobs = True
294:                     log_debug("[_handle_uppipe_client] Nhận CANCEL_TRANSFER từ Clipboard Agent.")
295:                     print("[Clipboard] Agent hủy truyền file (nút Hủy).")
296:                     self.cancel_active_transfer(remote_triggered=False, paste_id=paste_id, all_jobs=all_jobs)
297:                     return
298:                 if raw.startswith("COPIED_TEXT|"):
299:                     parts = raw.split("|", 1)
300:                     text = ""
301:                     try:
302:                         text = json.loads(parts[1]) if len(parts) > 1 else ""
303:                     except Exception:
304:                         text = parts[1] if len(parts) > 1 else ""
305:                     if text and self.active_sockets:
306:                         self._forward_clipboard_text(text)
307:                         print("[Clipboard] Host→client: đã gửi text từ Clipboard Agent.")
308:                     return
309:                 # Agent hỏi ghi đè — đưa vào packet loop (headless forward → client GUI).
310:                 if raw.startswith("CONFLICT_QUERY|"):
311:                     try:
312:                         payload = json.loads(raw.split("|", 1)[1])
313:                     except Exception as e:
314:                         log_debug(f"[_handle_uppipe_client] CONFLICT_QUERY parse: {e}")
315:                         return
316:                     try:
317:                         log_overwrite_conflict(
318:                             f"HOST_HEADLESS UpPipe CONFLICT_QUERY → enqueue "
319:                             f"paste_id={payload.get('paste_id')} "
320:                             f"n={len(payload.get('conflicts') or [])}"
321:                         )
322:                     except Exception:
323:                         pass
324:                     self.enqueue_packet({
325:                         "type": "paste_conflict_query",
326:                         "paste_id": payload.get("paste_id"),
327:                         "conflicts": payload.get("conflicts") or [],
328:                         "has_multiple": bool(payload.get("has_multiple")),
329:                     })
330:                     return
331:                 # Clipboard Agent gửi REQUEST_FILES khi người dùng thực hiện Paste
332:                 if raw.startswith("REQUEST_FILES"):
333:                     log_debug("[_handle_uppipe_client] Nhận REQUEST_FILES từ Clipboard Agent. Bắt đầu tải file...")
334:                     print("[Clipboard] Clipboard Agent yêu cầu tải file (người dùng đã Paste).")
335:                     try:
336:                         log_overwrite_conflict(
337:                             f"HOST_HEADLESS recv REQUEST_FILES raw_len={len(raw)} "
338:                             f"(neu truoc do khong co DIALOG SHOWN=YES / conflict_check → bug)"
339:                         )
340:                     except Exception:
341:                         pass
342:                     
343:                     parts = raw.split("|", 1)
344:                     requested_files = []
345:                     dest_dir = None
346:                     paste_id = None
347:                     if len(parts) > 1 and parts[1].strip():
348:                         try:
349:                             import json
350:                             payload = json.loads(parts[1])
351:                             if isinstance(payload, dict):
352:                                 requested_files = payload.get("files") or []
353:                                 dest_dir = payload.get("dest_dir") or None
354:                                 paste_id = payload.get("paste_id")
355:                             elif isinstance(payload, list):
356:                                 requested_files = payload
357:                         except Exception as e:
358:                             log_debug(f"[_handle_uppipe_client] Lỗi parse requested_files: {e}")
359:                     if paste_id is not None:
360:                         try:
361:                             self._clipboard_paste_id = int(paste_id)
362:                         except Exception:
363:                             pass
364:                             
365:                     if not requested_files:
366:                         requested_files = self.pending_remote_files
367: 
368:                     if requested_files:
369:                         tok = int(getattr(self, "_clipboard_paste_id", 0) or 0)
370:                         if tok in (getattr(self, "_cancelled_paste_ids", None) or set()):
371:                             print(f"[FileTransfer] Bo REQUEST_FILES paste_id={tok} (blacklist Huy).")
372:                             return
373:                         files_req = list(requested_files)
374:                         now = time.time()
375:                         last_req_time = float(getattr(self, "_last_request_files_time", 0.0) or 0.0)
376:                         last_req_tok = getattr(self, "_last_request_files_tok", None)
377:                         last_req_files = getattr(self, "_last_request_files_list", None)
378:                         if (tok and tok == last_req_tok and (now - last_req_time) < 5.0) or (
379:                             (now - last_req_time) < 10.0 and files_req == last_req_files
380:                         ):
381:                             log_debug(
382:                                 f"[_handle_uppipe_client] Bo REQUEST_FILES trung lap tok={tok} (elapsed={now - last_req_time:.2f}s)."
383:                             )
384:                             print(f"[FileTransfer] Bo REQUEST_FILES trung lap tok={tok} (elapsed={now - last_req_time:.2f}s).")
385:                             return
386:                         self._last_request_files_time = now
387:                         self._last_request_files_tok = tok
388:                         self._last_request_files_list = files_req
389:                         expected = getattr(self, "_xfer_expected_files", None)
390:                         if expected is None:
391:                             self._xfer_expected_files = {}
392:                             expected = self._xfer_expected_files
393:                         if tok:
394:                             expected[tok] = files_req
395:                         # Client→host: nhận vào staging. KHÔNG gắn dest Explorer vào
396:                         # job nhận — nếu gắn, promote/relocate sẽ ghi vào đích trước khi
397:                         # agent hỏi Replace. Agent đã có job["dest"] + replace_keys.
398:                         if dest_dir and os.path.isdir(dest_dir) and not _is_transfer_staging_dir(dest_dir):
399:                             log_debug(
400:                                 f"[_handle_uppipe_client] dest ghi nhan paste_id={tok}: {dest_dir} "
401:                                 f"(giu staging, khong promote)"
402:                             )
403:                         living = bool(getattr(self, "_xfer_jobs", None))
404:                         if not living:
405:                             self.target_save_dir = HEADLESS_TRANSFER_DIR if (
406:                                 self.app and getattr(self.app, "is_headless", False)
407:                             ) else getattr(self, "target_save_dir", None)
408:                         # Không ghi đè pending_remote_files — đó là copy mới nhất trên clipboard, không phải file đang Paste.
409:                         # Đây là nhận file (agent Paste trên host), không arm send-id — tránh phantom _active_send_ids.
410:                         self._allow_file_xfer = True
411:                         self._send_cancelled = False
412:                         self._receive_cancelled = False
413:                         threading.Thread(
414:                             target=self.request_pending_files,
415:                             args=(files_req, tok),
416:                             daemon=True,
417:                         ).start()
418:                     else:
419:                         log_debug("[_handle_uppipe_client] Không có pending_remote_files để tải.")
420:                         if self.app and getattr(self.app, 'is_headless', False):
421:                             self._send_progress_signal("CANCEL", "")
422:                             self._close_transfer_pipe()
423:                 else:
424:                     # Metadata file do Clipboard Agent gửi lên (copy file từ phía user)
425:                     if raw.startswith("COPIED_FILES|"):
426:                         parts = raw.split("|", 1)
427:                         offer_id = None
428:                         paths = json.loads(parts[1]) if len(parts) > 1 else []
429:                         if isinstance(paths, dict):
430:                             offer_id = paths.get("offer_id")
431:                             paths = paths.get("files") or []
432:                         metadata = []
433:                         for f in paths:
434:                             if os.path.isfile(f):
435:                                 metadata.append({
436:                                     "name": os.path.basename(f),
437:                                     "path": f,
438:                                     "size": os.path.getsize(f),
439:                                     "mtime": os.path.getmtime(f)
440:                                 })
441:                             elif os.path.isdir(f):
442:                                 parent_dir = os.path.dirname(f)
443:                                 for root, _, files in os.walk(f):
444:                                     for file in files:
445:                                         full_path = os.path.join(root, file)
446:                                         rel_path = os.path.relpath(full_path, parent_dir).replace('\\', '/')
447:                                         metadata.append({
448:                                             "name": rel_path,
449:                                             "path": full_path,
450:                                             "size": os.path.getsize(full_path),
451:                                             "mtime": os.path.getmtime(full_path)
452:                                         })
453:                     else:
454:                         metadata = json.loads(raw)
455:                         offer_id = None
456:                     if metadata and self.active_sockets:
457:                         self._clip_offer_id = int(getattr(self, "_clip_offer_id", 0) or 0) + 1
458:                         oid = int(offer_id or 0) or self._clip_offer_id
459:                         names = [m.get("name") for m in metadata[:8]]
460:                         try:
461:                             from utils.logger import clip_trace
462:                             clip_trace(f"[host] SEND files_copied_meta offer={oid} n={len(metadata)} {names}")
463:                         except Exception:
464:                             pass
465:                         pkt = json.dumps({
466:                             "type": "files_copied_meta",
467:                             "files": metadata,
468:                             "offer_id": oid,
469:                         }).encode('utf-8')
470:                         with self.lock:
471:                             for s in list(self.active_sockets):
472:                                 try: send_msg(s, pkt)
473:                                 except: pass
474:                         print(f"[Clipboard] Host→client files_copied_meta offer={oid}: {names}")
475:         except Exception as e:
476:             log_debug(f"[_handle_uppipe_client] Lỗi: {e}")
477:         finally:
478:             try:
479:                 win32file.CloseHandle(pipe_handle)
480:             except: pass

482: 
483:     def _send_progress_signal(self, data_type, payload):
484:         """PROGRESS/FILES/CANCEL đi cùng pipe persistent xuống agent."""
485:         self._send_to_pipe(data_type, payload)
486: 
487:     def _close_transfer_pipe(self):
488:         # Không ngắt pipe sau mỗi lần tải — agent giữ kết nối để PENDING lần sau hiện Paste ngay.
489:         return
490: 
491:     def _send_to_pipe(self, data_type, payload):
492:         """Gửi TYPE:payload xuống Clipboard Agent qua pipe giữ kết nối."""
493:         self._ensure_down_pipe()
494:         self._down_pipe_q.put((data_type, payload))
495:         log_debug(f"[_send_to_pipe] Hàng đợi {data_type} ({len(str(payload))} bytes).")
496:         if data_type == "PENDING":
497:             try:
498:                 log_overwrite_conflict(
499:                     f"HOST_HEADLESS queue PENDING to agent ({len(str(payload))} bytes)"
500:                 )
501:             except Exception:
502:                 pass
503: 
504:     def _repush_pending_offer_to_agent(self, reason=""):
505:         """Gửi lại PENDING xuống agent nếu headless đang giữ offer client→host."""
506:         if not (self.app and getattr(self.app, "is_headless", False)):
507:             return
508:         files = list(
509:             getattr(self, "pending_remote_files", None)
510:             or getattr(self, "_reoffer_files", None)
511:             or []
512:         )
513:         if not files:
514:             try:
515:                 log_overwrite_conflict(f"HOST_HEADLESS repush skip (no offer) reason={reason}")
516:             except Exception:
517:                 pass
518:             return
519:         total_size = sum((f.get("size", 0) if isinstance(f, dict) else 0) for f in files)
520:         item_kind, display_name = _copy_batch_kind_and_name(files, _("Tệp tin"))
521:         oid = int(getattr(self, "_clip_offer_applied", 0) or 0)
522:         msg_dict = {
523:             "display_name": display_name,
524:             "item_kind": item_kind,
525:             "total_size": total_size,
526:             "files": files,
527:             "offer_id": oid,
528:         }
529:         names = [f.get("name") if isinstance(f, dict) else str(f) for f in files[:8]]
530:         try:
531:             log_overwrite_conflict(
532:                 f"HOST_HEADLESS REPUSH PENDING reason={reason} offer={oid} "
533:                 f"n={len(files)} names={names}"
534:             )
535:         except Exception:
536:             pass
537:         self._send_to_pipe("PENDING", json.dumps(msg_dict))
538: 
539:     def _ensure_down_pipe(self):
540:         if getattr(self, "_down_pipe_started", False):
541:             return
542:         self._down_pipe_started = True
543:         threading.Thread(target=self._down_pipe_loop, daemon=True, name="ClipDownPipe").start()
544: 
545:     def _down_pipe_loop(self):
546:         """Giữ 1 kết nối Named Pipe xuống agent — không tạo/đóng pipe mỗi lần PENDING (trễ 0.5–1.5s)."""
547:         while True:
548:             pipe_handle = None
549:             try:
550:                 pipe_handle = create_named_pipe_with_everyone_dacl()
551:                 if pipe_handle is None or pipe_handle == -1:
552:                     time.sleep(0.15)
553:                     continue
554:                 log_debug("[_down_pipe_loop] Đang chờ Clipboard Agent kết nối...")
555:                 try:
556:                     win32pipe.ConnectNamedPipe(pipe_handle, None)
557:                 except Exception as ce:
558:                     err = getattr(ce, "winerror", 0) or (ce.args[0] if ce.args else 0)
559:                     if err != 535:
560:                         raise
561:                 log_debug("[_down_pipe_loop] Agent đã kết nối. Giữ pipe để gửi PENDING/TEXT.")
562:                 try:
563:                     log_overwrite_conflict("HOST_HEADLESS DownPipe AGENT CONNECTED — flush PENDING if any")
564:                 except Exception:
565:                     pass
566:                 # Agent mới (sau cài/restart) — gửi lại offer đang giữ, tránh paste client→host mất PENDING.
567:                 try:
568:                     self._repush_pending_offer_to_agent("down_pipe_connected")
569:                 except Exception as re:
570:                     log_debug(f"[_down_pipe_loop] repush PENDING: {re}")
571:                 while True:
572:                     data_type, payload = self._down_pipe_q.get()
573:                     msg = f"{data_type}:{payload}\x00"
574:                     try:
575:                         win32file.WriteFile(pipe_handle, msg.encode("utf-8"))
576:                         if data_type == "PENDING":
577:                             try:
578:                                 log_overwrite_conflict(
579:                                     f"HOST_HEADLESS WriteFile PENDING OK ({len(msg)} bytes) → agent"
580:                                 )
581:                             except Exception:
582:                                 pass
583:                     except Exception as we:
584:                         log_debug(f"[_down_pipe_loop] WriteFile lỗi: {we}")
585:                         try:
586:                             log_overwrite_conflict(f"HOST_HEADLESS WriteFile {data_type} FAIL: {we}")
587:                         except Exception:
588:                             pass
589:                         try:
590:                             self._down_pipe_q.put((data_type, payload))
591:                         except Exception:
592:                             pass
593:                         break
594:             except Exception as e:
595:                 log_debug(f"[_down_pipe_loop] {e}")
596:             finally:
597:                 if pipe_handle is not None and pipe_handle != -1:
598:                     try:
599:                         win32file.FlushFileBuffers(pipe_handle)
600:                     except Exception:
601:                         pass
602:                     try:
603:                         win32pipe.DisconnectNamedPipe(pipe_handle)
604:                     except Exception:
605:                         pass
606:                     try:
607:                         win32file.CloseHandle(pipe_handle)
608:                     except Exception:
609:                         pass
610:             time.sleep(0.05)
611: 
612:     def _send_path_to_pipe(self, file_path):
613:         """
614:         Gửi đường dẫn file qua Named Pipe cho Clipboard Agent.
615:         """
616:         self._send_to_pipe("FILE", file_path)
617: 
618:     def _schedule_copied_files_sync(self):
619:         """Đọc file list sau khi seq clipboard ổn định; không gửi list cũ khi user đã copy file mới."""
620:         gen = getattr(self, "_clip_sync_gen", 0) + 1
621:         self._clip_sync_gen = gen
622:         old = getattr(self, "_clip_sync_timer", None)
623:         if old is not None:
624:             try:
625:                 old.cancel()
626:             except Exception:
627:                 pass
628: 
629:         is_client = bool(getattr(self, "pygame_hwnd", None))
630: 
631:         def _pass(g=gen, tries=0):
632:             if g != getattr(self, "_clip_sync_gen", 0):
633:                 return
634:             seq_before = get_clipboard_sequence_number()
635:             # Client ưu tiên CIDA; nếu rỗng thì đọc HDROP (Win11 Explorer đôi khi thiếu Shell IDList).
636:             files = get_clipboard_files(retries=1, allow_hdrop=not is_client)
637:             if is_client and not files:
638:                 files = get_clipboard_files(retries=2, allow_hdrop=True)
639:             seq_after = get_clipboard_sequence_number()
640:             if seq_before and seq_after and seq_before != seq_after and tries < 4:
641:                 t = threading.Timer(0.12, lambda: _pass(g, tries + 1))
642:                 t.daemon = True
643:                 t.start()
644:                 self._clip_sync_timer = t
645:                 return
646:             if files:
647:                 fp = tuple(os.path.abspath(f).lower() for f in files)
648:                 last_fp = getattr(self, "_last_sent_file_fp", None)
649:                 last_seq = getattr(self, "_last_sent_file_seq", None)
650:                 # Trùng file + chưa đổi seq clipboard → không gửi lại (tránh offer 2,3,4 spam).
651:                 if last_fp and fp == last_fp and (not seq_after or seq_after == last_seq):
652:                     return
653:                 if last_fp and fp == last_fp and seq_after and seq_after != last_seq and tries < 4:
654:                     t = threading.Timer(0.12, lambda: _pass(g, tries + 1))
655:                     t.daemon = True
656:                     t.start()

1824:     def _send_cancel_to_host(paste_id=None):
1825:         """Báo worker dừng gửi/nhận. Event Global dễ fail (SYSTEM vs Medium IL)."""

1129:     """
1130:     Đánh giá tọa độ click Win11 (Dành riêng cho click vùng trống).
1131:     Bắt Icon Paste ở đỉnh/đáy và Text Paste ở giữa.

819: def _hwnd_is_context_menu(hwnd):
820:     """Cửa sổ (hoặc cha) là menu chuột phải — không gồm khung Explorer chính."""
821:     user32 = ctypes.windll.user32
822:     seen = set()
823:     cur = hwnd
824:     for _ in range(12):
825:         if not cur:
826:             break
827:         try:
828:             key = int(cur)
829:         except Exception:
830:             break
831:         if key in seen:
832:             break
833:         seen.add(key)
834:         cls = _hwnd_class_name(cur)
835:         if cls in _CONTEXT_MENU_HIT_CLASSES:
836:             # CabinetWClass / ExploreWClass không bao giờ là menu.
837:             if cls in ("CabinetWClass", "ExploreWClass", "WorkerW", "Progman"):
838:                 return False
839:             return True
840:         if cls in (
841:             "Microsoft.UI.Content.DesktopChildSiteBridge",
842:             "Windows.UI.Composition.DesktopWindowContentBridge",
843:         ):
844:             try:
845:                 user32.GetWindowLongW.restype = ctypes.c_long
846:                 style = int(user32.GetWindowLongW(ctypes.c_void_p(cur), -16) or 0)
847:                 ex = int(user32.GetWindowLongW(ctypes.c_void_p(cur), -20) or 0)
848:             except Exception:
849:                 style, ex = 0, 0
850:             ws_popup = bool(style & 0x80000000)
851:             ws_caption = bool(style & 0x00C00000)
852:             ws_ex_tool = bool(ex & 0x00000080)
853:             ws_child = bool(style & 0x40000000)
854:             # Win11 XAML: item menu thường là child bridge trong popup host.
855:             if ws_popup and (ws_ex_tool or not ws_caption):
856:                 return True
857:             if ws_child and not ws_caption:
858:                 # Leo thêm — nếu tổ tiên là XamlExplorerHostIslandWindow thì là menu.
859:                 pass
860:         try:
861:             user32.GetAncestor.restype = wintypes.HWND
862:             nxt = user32.GetAncestor(ctypes.c_void_p(cur), 1)
863:             if not nxt or nxt == cur:
864:                 user32.GetWindow.restype = wintypes.HWND
865:                 nxt = user32.GetWindow(ctypes.c_void_p(cur), 4)
866:             cur = nxt
867:         except Exception:
868:             break
869:     return False

========== MODEL ==========
Đã tìm ra chính xác các nguyên nhân gây ra hiện tượng **trên Windows 11 bị delay, lúc chạy lúc không, phải paste vài lần mới ăn**, trong khi **Windows 10 lại chạy mượt không bị trễ**. Toàn bộ vấn đề đã được khắc phục triệt để trong file [filecopyhost.py](file:///d:/Data/AI%20Code/remote_desktop/filecopyhost.py).

---

### 🔍 Nguyên nhân gốc rễ (Root Causes)

1. **Bug logic nhận diện menu XAML của Windows 11 trong [`_agent_lmb_is_context_paste`](file:///d:/Data/AI%20Code/remote_desktop/filecopyhost.py#L1490-L1545):**
   - Trên **Windows 10**, menu chuột phải là Win32 `#32768` cổ điển, khi click sau chuột phải thì hàm fallback `_win10_rmb_paste_click` luôn trả về `True` $\rightarrow$ ghi nhận gesture Paste thành công ngay lập tức.
   - Trên **Windows 11**, menu chuột phải là XAML Island hiện đại. Khi click menu, hàm phân loại trả về `kind = "unknown"` hoặc `name = "[xaml-not-paste]"` (do tọa độ lệch thanh icon command bar). 
   - Đoạn code xử lý Windows 11 trước đây bị đặt **lồng sai bên trong block `if _menu_item_counts_as_paste(kind):`** (vốn chỉ nhận `kind == "paste"`), đồng thời nhánh `not _is_windows_11()` loại trừ Win 11 $\rightarrow$ dẫn đến **100% cú click trên menu Win 11 bị trả về `False`**.
   - Khi đó, biến `_agent_paste_gesture_id` không tăng, gesture Paste không được ghi nhận.

2. **Windows 11 bị loại khỏi fallback trong `WM_RENDERFORMAT`:**
   - Trong `WM_RENDERFORMAT`, biến `recent_paste_click` và `fresh_input` phụ thuộc vào `_win10_rmb_paste_click(...)`. Hàm này ngay dòng đầu tiên đã hardcode: `if _is_windows_11(): return False`.
   - Kết quả là trên Win 11, khi Explorer gửi `WM_RENDERFORMAT`, hệ thống **tưởng nhầm đây là Explorer đang query menu/probe ngầm** chứ không phải user bấm Paste $\rightarrow$ **trả về dummy HDROP rỗng** và không tải file! User phải bấm lại vài lần chờ tình huống menu đóng trước poll để rơi vào nhánh cứu nguy thì mới chạy được.

3. **Cơ chế chặn nhầm thao tác Paste của người dùng (`_agent_suppress_render_until` & cooldown 3.5s):**
   - Khi Win 11 mở context menu, Explorer thăm dò clipboard làm kích hoạt dummy probe $\rightarrow$ set `_agent_dummy_h_active = True`.
   - Ngay khi user bấm chuột để Paste, poll phát hiện `_agent_dummy_h_active` và gọi `WM_USER_SETUP_DELAYED`, set thời gian hoãn `_agent_suppress_render_until = time.time() + 2.0s`.
   - Khi Explorer yêu cầu định dạng `CF_HDROP` để dán, code kiểm tra `time.time() < _agent_suppress_render_until` **trước khi kiểm tra xem user có vừa bấm Ctrl+V hay click Paste hay không**, dẫn đến chặn đứng lệnh Paste của user và gọi `_agent_schedule_rearm(0.25)` kéo dài thêm 2.75s!
   - Thêm vào đó, thời gian cooldown duplicate bị set quá lớn (3.5 giây), khiến mọi lần bấm Paste tiếp theo trong vòng 3.5s đều bị drop sạch.

---

### 🛠️ Các cải tiến đã thực hiện trong [filecopyhost.py](file:///d:/Data/AI%20Code/remote_desktop/filecopyhost.py)

1. **Thêm hàm nhận diện thống nhất `_is_recent_rmb_paste_click_unified`:**
   - Với Windows 10: Giữ nguyên logic gọi `_win10_rmb_paste_click`.
   - Với Windows 11: Cho phép click chuột trái sau chuột phải từ 0.12s đến 8.0s trong vùng menu hoặc Explorer được nhận diện chuẩn xác là thao tác Paste (bỏ qua các mã giả `[xaml-not-paste]`).

2. **Sửa logic [`_agent_lmb_is_context_paste`](file:///d:/Data/AI%20Code/remote_desktop/filecopyhost.py#L1490-L1580):**
   - Đưa nhánh xử lý Windows 11 ra ngoài độc lập: nếu click trên menu sau khi RMB thì xác định là `paste` và ghi nhận gesture ngay.
   - Bổ sung nhận diện nút Paste trên **Command Bar (thanh lệnh trên cùng của File Explorer Win 11)** thông qua `_cursor_in_explorer_command_bar()`.

3. **Tối ưu luồng `WM_RENDERFORMAT`:**
   - Ưu tiên tính `is_user_paste = recent_ctrl or recent_paste_click`. Nếu đúng là user đang Paste (bằng Ctrl+V hoặc click chuột), **ngay lập tức xóa cờ chặn `_agent_suppress_render_until = 0.0`** và hủy bỏ trạng thái `is_menu`, đưa luồng dán file vào thực thi ngay lập tức không trễ 1 mili-giây nào.
   - Chỉ áp dụng suppress HDROP rỗng đối với các tiến trình probe ngầm thực sự.

4. **Giảm debounce từ 3.5s xuống mức nhạy 0.5s - 0.8s:**
   - Giúp việc paste nhiều lần, paste liên tục giữa các thư mục diễn ra mượt mà, phản hồi tức thì và không bị nuốt lệnh.

5. **Tránh Setup Delayed đè khi đang Paste trong `_agent_mouse_poll_loop` và `_stamp_lbutton`:**
   - Chỉ gửi `WM_USER_SETUP_DELAYED` khi cú click chuột **không phải là click Paste**.

214: def merge_full_service(app_dist_dir):
215:     """Ghép RemoteDesktopService.exe + deps thiếu vào app.dist (giống slim merge của Host)."""
216:     service_dist_dir = os.path.join(workspace_dir, "dist_nuitka_service", "windows_service_loop.dist")
217:     if not os.path.exists(service_dist_dir):
218:         return False
219:     svc_src = os.path.join(service_dist_dir, "windows_service_loop.exe")
220:     svc_dst = os.path.join(app_dist_dir, "RemoteDesktopService.exe")
221:     if not os.path.exists(svc_src):
222:         log("ERROR: windows_service_loop.exe missing in service dist.")
223:         return False
224:     log("Merging RemoteDesktopService.exe (slim — only missing runtime files)...")
225:     shutil.copy2(svc_src, svc_dst)
226:     for item in os.listdir(service_dist_dir):
227:         if item.lower() == "windows_service_loop.exe":
228:             continue
229:         s = os.path.join(service_dist_dir, item)
230:         d = os.path.join(app_dist_dir, item)
231:         if os.path.exists(d):
232:             continue
233:         try:
234:             if os.path.isdir(s):
235:                 shutil.copytree(s, d)
236:             else:
237:                 shutil.copy2(s, d)
238:         except Exception as e:
239:             log(f"Warning: Failed to merge service file {item}: {e}")
240:     try_authenticode_sign(svc_dst)
241:     log("Added RemoteDesktopService.exe")
242:     return True

246:     try:
247:         total = 0

252:                 except Exception:
253:                     pass
254:         log(f"{label}: {total / (1024 * 1024):.1f} MB")
255:     except Exception as e:
256:         log(f"Could not measure {label}: {e}")

261:         if os.path.exists(path):
262:             log(f"{label}: {os.path.getsize(path) / (1024 * 1024):.1f} MB ({path})")

267: def shorten_openblas_dll(app_dist_dir):
268:     """Đổi tên DLL OpenBLAS do NumPy đóng gói (tên hash dài) thành libopenblas.dll và vá import trong .pyd."""
269:     import re
270:     short_name = "libopenblas.dll"
271:     long_path = None
272:     long_name = None
273:     try:
274:         names = os.listdir(app_dist_dir)
275:     except Exception as e:
276:         log(f"Không đọc được {app_dist_dir}: {e}")
277:         return
278:     for name in names:
279:         if re.match(r"libopenblas\..+\.dll$", name, re.I) and name.lower() != short_name:
280:             long_path = os.path.join(app_dist_dir, name)
281:             long_name = name
282:             break
283:     if not long_path:
284:         log("No hashed libopenblas DLL found to rename.")
285:         return
286:     old_bytes = long_name.encode("ascii")
287:     short_bytes = short_name.encode("ascii")
288:     if len(short_bytes) >= len(old_bytes):
289:         log(f"Short name {short_name} is not shorter; skip.")
290:         return
291:     padded = short_bytes + b"\x00" * (len(old_bytes) - len(short_bytes))
292:     patched = 0
293:     for root, _dirs, files in os.walk(app_dist_dir):
294:         for f in files:
295:             if not f.lower().endswith((".dll", ".pyd", ".exe")):
296:                 continue
297:             path = os.path.join(root, f)
298:             try:
299:                 with open(path, "rb") as fh:
300:                     data = fh.read()
301:             except Exception:
302:                 continue
303:             if old_bytes not in data:
304:                 continue
305:             data = data.replace(old_bytes, padded)
306:             with open(path, "wb") as fh:
307:                 fh.write(data)
308:             patched += 1
309:             log(f"Patched OpenBLAS import in {os.path.relpath(path, app_dist_dir)}")
310:     dest = os.path.join(app_dist_dir, short_name)
311:     if os.path.abspath(dest) != os.path.abspath(long_path):
312:         if os.path.exists(dest):
313:             try:
314:                 os.remove(dest)
315:             except Exception as e:
316:                 log(f"Could not remove old {short_name}: {e}")
317:                 return
318:         os.rename(long_path, dest)
319:     log(f"Renamed {long_name} -> {short_name} (patched {patched} files).")
320: 
321: def try_authenticode_sign(exe_path):
322:     """Ký Authenticode nếu máy có signtool + chứng chỉ code signing (cách đúng để giảm Defender ML)."""
323:     if not os.path.exists(exe_path):
324:         return
325:     signtool = None
326:     kit = os.path.join(os.environ.get("ProgramFiles(x86)", r"C:\Program Files (x86)"), "Windows Kits", "10", "bin")
327:     if os.path.isdir(kit):
328:         for root, _dirs, files in os.walk(kit):
329:             if "signtool.exe" in files and os.path.basename(root).lower() == "x64":
330:                 signtool = os.path.join(root, "signtool.exe")
331:     if not signtool:
332:         log("Không tìm thấy signtool.exe — bỏ qua ký Authenticode. Defender ML có thể vẫn cảnh báo installer chưa ký.")
333:         log("Cách xử lý: chứng chỉ code signing, hoặc gửi file lên https://www.microsoft.com/en-us/wdsi/filesubmission")
334:         return
335:     cmd = f'"{signtool}" sign /a /fd SHA256 /tr http://timestamp.digicert.com /td SHA256 "{exe_path}"'
336:     if run_cmd(cmd):
337:         log(f"Đã ký Authenticode: {exe_path}")
338:     else:
339:         log("Không ký được (chưa có chứng chỉ code signing). Gửi Microsoft WDSI nếu Defender cảnh báo.")
340:

342:     main()
